Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The leptons variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 123 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 266 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 123 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the leptons structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 266. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
